# Missing laws: the fetch list for Phase 6

This file is generated by [`scripts/missing_laws.py`](../scripts/missing_laws.py). Don't edit it by hand.

Our 5,740 labour chunks mention numbered laws and decrees 4,927 times. 2,211 of those mentions point to 413 laws we have. The other 2,716 point to **700 laws that are not a document in our data**. They are listed below, most mentioned first.

274 of the missing laws are in another volume of the same dataset, so they can be fetched without looking anywhere else. The last column names the volume. For the rest we need another source.

**468 documents in our data are only a title**, or a title and an editorial note. Their rules aren't in the data either, so they belong on the fetch list too (second table).

## How this was counted

- **What counts as a mention:** Ν., Ν.Δ., Α.Ν., Π.Δ. and Β.Δ. followed by a number, in any of the forms the texts use (`Νόμ. 435/1976`, `Ν.Δ. 4020/59`, `Νόμ. 2556/23-24 Δεκ. 1997`, `υπ’ αριθ. 748/66 Β.Δ/τος`, `ν. 2112`). Words repeated by overlapping chunks are counted once.
- **What is left out:** ministerial decisions (Υ.Α., Κ.Υ.Α.); decrees cited only by date (`Β.Δ. 16/18 Ιουλ. 1920`), because the first number is a day; laws numbered in Greek letters (`ν. ΔΛ΄`).
- **Same law:** type and number match and the year is at most 1 away, because a law may be voted one year and published the next. Mentions without a year take the year of a matching document, or the only year other mentions give. If no year is known and the number is under 100, the mention is dropped, because too many such matches aren't laws.
- **Printing errors stay as written:** for example `Ν. 4435/1976`, a misprint of Ν. 435/1976, gets its own row. So a few rows are not real laws.
- **Other volumes:** found by reading the header of every document in the other 46 volumes. A blank cell doesn't prove a law is absent, because some headers can't be read.
- **Title only:** a document of one chunk with nothing after its title, or only an editorial note such as `Κατηργήθη δια …`. A one-sentence decision whose whole content is its title counts too.
